Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a seismology algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 244. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 684/3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 244 + O(1/n^684). Summing over all modes and applying the seismology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
